- **How parallel class-high branches merge (working convention).** The merge gate covers a head only when X..head is class normal for the verdict's reviewed commit X, and merging `main` into a branch after X brings `main`'s own class-high changes into X..head. So an item branch's judged commits carry neither `DURUM.md`, `plan/ledger.md` nor a log entry; after its verdict the branch adds only the verdict file and, if it rendered the views earlier, sets them back to its base; the log entry, the item's acceptance and the render go in a records pull request right after the merge. Between the two merges `render --check` on `main` reports the views as not current; `tools/stop_check.sh` is run only after the records pull request.
